The sensitive moment is the **next paste**, not only the investor deck. Consumer terms with a lab are **not an NDA**. Coding agents leak more than chat (corrections, tool traces, tests, evals). This skill does **not** watch every tool and does **not** see the lab train; human review stays the trust layer. This skill **cannot prove or prevent training**. - **`--always-approve` / `--yolo` is not owner approval** for send, pay, identity, DNS, or voice/phone provision. A compact is not a wipe (SessionStart `clear`/`compact`, including mid-turn, delivers additionalContext to the immediate continuation). A PostToolUse block is not a rollback. Recrawl 4 Oct: `additionalContextLimit` on an event that cannot produce additional context is ignored and warns — a limit on the wrong event is not a vault and not a wipe. Compact, block, and parsed-but-skipped stand. This skill still ships no hooks. Without `--bare`, `claude -p` runs project hooks and connects `.mcp.json` even in a folder never trusted (no trust dialog). Before v2.1.286, bare limits held only partly (interactive bare still connected MCP; every bare session sent system reminders; background tasks stayed available). Bare is still not a vault. @ SKILL.md MOVED-TO-REF references/shared-bot-computer.md 4178
- **Shared Bot computer is account-wide.** All of your Bots share files, browser sessions, and app logins. Treat anything on that computer as available to every Bot you run. Isolation between users is strict; isolation between your Bots is not. Livestream/invite copy that each Bot has its own computer is marketing, not isolation. **Screens are separate work surfaces, not security boundaries.** Do not use separate Bots as a vault. Isolated credentials need a **dedicated user**. Delete Bot ≠ wipe computer files/sign-ins. Enterprise terminate-computer keeps the durable disk (next session remounts it). **Bot-to-Bot DM / group chat is a disclosure hop.** Auto Review does not review memory writes / most settings changes. **Grok Bot computers run in the United States today** — not on-prem, not BYO image, not inside your perimeter; holdbacks on that computer are US-hosted. Recreate (image update / policy) can drop in-computer sign-in sessions; terminate-computer still keeps the durable disk. **Sign-out ≠ wipe:** Claude Code 2.1.272 keeps claude.ai-synced skills on disk until `cleanupPeriodDays`, then trash. **v2.1.273+** signed-in terminal sessions download to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` then check claude.ai **~every 10 minutes** and apply add/edit/disable **without a restart** (synced copies are **read-only** and download **on invoke**; **bare mode** and **safe mode** skip sync) (v2.1.275 also plugins; opt-out `syncClaudeAiSkills` / `syncClaudeAiPlugins`). **Custom commands have been merged into skills** (`.claude/commands/foo.md` ≡ `.claude/skills/foo/SKILL.md` → `/foo`; existing commands keep working). **Stacked slash-skills** load the first skill plus up to five more (`/a /b /c do X` — trailing text is `$ARGUMENTS` to each); chaining this skill with a send/publish skill is a disclosure multiplier. v2.1.277: a project with no `CLAUDE.md` reads `AGENTS.md` instead — that file is a hop if it holds method. `/config` can load **both** AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md — both are hops. Grok also discovers user-level `~/.agents/commands/`. A marketplace skill that lets a Bot take jobs or get paid is access grant + send + computer-use. claude.ai enablement now syncs skills to Cowork/cloud; `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS=1` downloads to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` (prefixed `/anthropic-skills:`). Local `~/.claude/skills/` still does not auto-sync to Cowork. Each enablement is a publish. `disable-model-invocation` also blocks scheduled-task skill prompts (v2.1.196+). Synced skills: no local `!` commands; frontmatter sanitized. **Desktop-egress is a CDN, not a vault** (Settings → Updates → Update Grok Bot's Computer FIRST, then Settings → Computer → Route Computer traffic through this desktop; reverse order errors "wasn't provisioned with the egress tunnel"; laptop closed stops routing; holdbacks on that desktop see Bot traffic). **Ownership-pass is access grant + hop** (Bots on one account hand off tasks on the shared computer; decline unnamed). **No Legacy Privacy Mode** (Grok Bot requires cloud storage; training opt-out follows the Cursor account, not a vault). Do not add `allowed-tools` write grants **or** `disallowed-tools` to this skill. **Grok `allowed-tools` does not grant or restrict** (ignored for tool policy — not a vault or a lock). Anthropic **`allowed-tools` / `disallowed-tools` are one-turn** — grant and restriction both clear on the next message; cannot remove `EndConversation` if other tools remain. One-turn grant-clear / restriction-clear is not a vault. **Dist-path:** Claude Code accepts all frontmatter; claude.ai / Skills API / `package_skill.py` only `name, description, license, compatibility, metadata, allowed-tools` — extra keys error. That is why `claude-skill/` is name+description only. **Skill security scanning is not a vault** (claude.ai/Cowork only; misses Skills API, already-uploaded skills, CMEK/ZDR/HIPAA orgs). Custom claude.ai Skills are per-user and do not auto-sync; Anthropic settings enablement may — neither is a vault. Listing truncate: combined description + when_to_use 1536 chars. Skill listing budget is 1% of context. `context: fork` is a hop (subagent does not inherit this skill unless listed).

Grok: **project** skills = `.grok/skills/` (not project `.agents/skills/`); user-level `~/.agents/skills/` **and** `~/.agents/commands/` **are** discovered; Teach-a-task **no mic** + keep write actions behind approval + secure credential handoff; cloud ≠ desktop-egress ≠ local-exec; laptop-closed stops egress routing not cloud work; Auto Review rules are desktop-local; no Legacy Privacy Mode; Cursor training opt-out; ownership-pass is a hop. @ chatgpt-skill/SKILL.md MOVED-TO-REF references/shared-bot-computer.md 4178
- **Shared Bot computer is account-wide.** All of your Bots share files, browser sessions, and app logins. Treat anything on that computer as available to every Bot you run. Isolation between users is strict; isolation between your Bots is not. Livestream/invite copy that each Bot has its own computer is marketing, not isolation. **Screens are separate work surfaces, not security boundaries.** Do not use separate Bots as a vault. Isolated credentials need a **dedicated user**. Delete Bot ≠ wipe computer files/sign-ins. Enterprise terminate-computer keeps the durable disk (next session remounts it). **Bot-to-Bot DM / group chat is a disclosure hop.** Auto Review does not review memory writes / most settings changes. **Grok Bot computers run in the United States today** — not on-prem, not BYO image, not inside your perimeter; holdbacks on that computer are US-hosted. One-turn grant-clear / restriction-clear is not a vault. **Dist-path:** Claude Code accepts all frontmatter; claude.ai / Skills API / `package_skill.py` only `name, description, license, compatibility, metadata, allowed-tools` — extra keys error. Skill listing budget is 1% of context. `context: fork` is a hop (subagent does not inherit this skill unless listed).

@ claude-skill/SKILL.md MOVED-TO-REF references/l3-callouts.md 52919
**L3 callouts (three frames — do not conflate):** (a) Friedberg All-In **hosts clip** (12 Sep): de-ID / unidentifiable data can absorb method novelty; the **approach is the IP**; anecdote ≠ audited proof; (b) Satya **blog** 12 Jul: exhaust/corrections; buyer pays twice — Satya **Summit LA** is privacy + weights I control + “My IP shouldn't leak” + exhaust ownership, **not** the de-ID→next-version claim; (c) ZDR is contractual best-efforts, not a vault. The sensitive moment is the **next paste**, not only the investor deck. Consumer terms with a lab are **not an NDA**. Coding agents leak more than chat (corrections, tool traces, tests, evals). This skill does **not** watch every tool and does **not** see the lab train; human review stays the trust layer. This skill **cannot prove or prevent training**. Load `references/friedberg-satya-hygiene.md`. Clip-only stays L0. Screenshot-first of client correspondence into consumer Extra High is a next-paste (CSAM-scanned + know-how); Extra High / model pick stays L0. SDK `skills=all` loads untrusted marketplace skills (a hop). Mailed HITL zip v0.5.25 is stale — testers use this branch. Standalone skills = desktop/CLI; Chat/Work web+mobile need a plugin (Hold; plugin.json spec is public — do not invent overnight). Disallow AI Training is not a vault. **Firm / no-train lane:** dropping a research memo into Extra High / “improve the model” consumer chat **demonstrates the problem while describing it** — hold that paste. GPT-5.5→gpt-5.6-sol is L0 model pick. `grok plugin install --trust` is a hop (SHA-pin ≠ vault). Grok Build **project** skills = `.grok/skills/` (does **not** scan project `.agents/skills/`; user-level `~/.agents/skills/` **is** discovered). Galaxy Day 2–3 / **finale** / remaining role livestreams / free-month usage-reset stay L0. Leftover UAT: **do-not-resend**. Remaining leftover drafts stay unsent. MCP Scan Tools snapshot = method paste; **universal directory = dual-surface** (Hold; host-stripped Claude/Cursor forks are a hop). Web install ≠ hook deploy. **Office add-in is a next-paste. Custom GPT → plugin is dual-surface (Hold). Record & Replay packaged as plugin = dual-surface. Teach-a-task (no mic; keep write actions behind approval; secure credential handoff) is demonstration-to-skill. Scan Tools rescan is another paste. Claude-plugin submit to OpenAI is dual-surface (approvals don't transfer; hooks don't run in ordinary Chat). Import-other-agent chats/skills/memories is a next-paste.** claude.ai terminal sync v2.1.273+ checks ~every 10 minutes without restart (v2.1.275 also plugins). Custom commands merged into skills. **Stacked slash-skills** (first + up to 5 more) is a disclosure multiplier. Claude-plugin submit: skills-only vs With MCP still dual-surface; userConfig / live artifacts / local MCP don't transfer. AGENTS.md fallback (no CLAUDE.md) is a hop. Custom GPT migrate option date has passed (was 22 Sep) — still Hold (freeze 26 Oct; retire 11 Dec); do not dump knowledge because the calendar flipped. Plugin lifecycle hooks run in the Codex runtime including ChatGPT Work and Codex — ordinary Chat is not that sentence; this skill still has none; do not add hooks so Work picks it up. Local marketplace install is the test path (direct, indirect, follow-up, negative, boundary); Secure MCP Tunnel does not replace the public HTTPS required for submission; published tool updates use continuous review but skill changes still need a new version. Mailed try pack v0.5.36 is stale — do not resend; testers use this branch. claude.ai synced skills are read-only and download on invoke; bare mode and safe mode skip sync. /config can load both AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md — both are hops. Private skills are one library shared by all Bots. Broad event listeners are a disclosure multiplier. An infra or access overview pasted into a public skill, tester zip, or consumer chat is a next-paste — no names. Weekly backup ≠ publish. @ claude-skill/SKILL.md MOVED-TO-REF references/shared-bot-computer.md 4178
- **Shared Bot computer is account-wide.** All of your Bots share files, browser sessions, and app logins. Treat anything on that computer as available to every Bot you run. Isolation between users is strict; isolation between your Bots is not. Livestream/invite copy that each Bot has its own computer is marketing, not isolation. **Screens are separate work surfaces, not security boundaries.** Do not use separate Bots as a vault. Isolated credentials need a **dedicated user**. Delete Bot ≠ wipe computer files/sign-ins. Enterprise terminate-computer keeps the durable disk (next session remounts it). **Bot-to-Bot DM / group chat is a disclosure hop.** Auto Review does not review memory writes / most settings changes. **Grok Bot computers run in the United States today** — not on-prem, not BYO image, not inside your perimeter; holdbacks on that computer are US-hosted. Recreate (image update / policy) can drop in-computer sign-in sessions; terminate-computer still keeps the durable disk. **Sign-out ≠ wipe:** Claude Code 2.1.272 keeps claude.ai-synced skills on disk until `cleanupPeriodDays`, then trash. **v2.1.273+** signed-in terminal sessions download to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` then check claude.ai **~every 10 minutes** and apply add/edit/disable **without a restart** (synced copies are **read-only** and download **on invoke**; **bare mode** and **safe mode** skip sync) (v2.1.275 also plugins; opt-out `syncClaudeAiSkills` / `syncClaudeAiPlugins`). **Custom commands have been merged into skills** (`.claude/commands/foo.md` ≡ `.claude/skills/foo/SKILL.md` → `/foo`; existing commands keep working). **Stacked slash-skills** load the first skill plus up to five more (`/a /b /c do X` — trailing text is `$ARGUMENTS` to each); chaining this skill with a send/publish skill is a disclosure multiplier. v2.1.277: a project with no `CLAUDE.md` reads `AGENTS.md` instead — that file is a hop if it holds method. `/config` can load **both** AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md — both are hops. Grok also discovers user-level `~/.agents/commands/`. A marketplace skill that lets a Bot take jobs or get paid is access grant + send + computer-use. claude.ai enablement now syncs skills to Cowork/cloud; `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS=1` downloads to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` (prefixed `/anthropic-skills:`). Local `~/.claude/skills/` still does not auto-sync to Cowork. Each enablement is a publish. `disable-model-invocation` also blocks scheduled-task skill prompts (v2.1.196+). Synced skills: no local `!` commands; frontmatter sanitized. **Desktop-egress is a CDN, not a vault** (Settings → Updates → Update Grok Bot's Computer FIRST, then Settings → Computer → Route Computer traffic through this desktop; reverse order errors "wasn't provisioned with the egress tunnel"; laptop closed stops routing; holdbacks on that desktop see Bot traffic). **Ownership-pass is access grant + hop** (Bots on one account hand off tasks on the shared computer; decline unnamed). **No Legacy Privacy Mode** (Grok Bot requires cloud storage; training opt-out follows the Cursor account, not a vault). Do not add `allowed-tools` write grants **or** `disallowed-tools` to this skill. **Grok `allowed-tools` does not grant or restrict** (ignored for tool policy — not a vault or a lock). Anthropic **`allowed-tools` / `disallowed-tools` are one-turn** — grant and restriction both clear on the next message; cannot remove `EndConversation` if other tools remain. One-turn grant-clear / restriction-clear is not a vault. **Dist-path:** Claude Code accepts all frontmatter; claude.ai / Skills API / `package_skill.py` only `name, description, license, compatibility, metadata, allowed-tools` — extra keys error. That is why `claude-skill/` is name+description only. **Skill security scanning is not a vault** (claude.ai/Cowork only; misses Skills API, already-uploaded skills, CMEK/ZDR/HIPAA orgs). Custom claude.ai Skills are per-user and do not auto-sync; Anthropic settings enablement may — neither is a vault. Listing truncate: combined description + when_to_use 1536 chars. Skill listing budget is 1% of context. `context: fork` is a hop (subagent does not inherit this skill unless listed).

OpenAI **plugins/build/skills** recrawl **2026-09-20** (Astra IF kept; Copilot **cloud** loads `.github/skills/` `.claude/skills/` `.agents/skills/` + `~/.copilot/skills/`; Genkit/marketplace `npx skills add` is a hop; Claude-plugin submit / import-other-agent / package-as-plugin dual-surface Holds kept. Record & Replay window content; Scan Tools rescan; Word add-in next-paste; custom GPT→plugin dual-surface Hold) (developers.openai.com/codex/skills + **api/docs/guides/tools-skills** + Anthropic Agent SDK skills + Grok skills-plugins-marketplaces): skills = authoring; plugins = distribution. Standalone skills = ChatGPT desktop + Codex CLI/IDE; Chat/Work web+mobile need a plugin (do not invent plugin.json / `.codex-plugin/plugin.json` overnight; Hold). SDK `skills="all"` is a hop. Grok extra YAML keys are ignored; Claude dist-path extra keys error. Prefer instructions over scripts. Every skill states input, steps, output, **facts not to infer**, **when to ask / stop / decline**, and **which supporting files to consult**. **Explicit user instructions this turn take precedence over skill guidelines** (explicit skill **requirements** still bind); named recipient AND action is that override. On L3 decline, **name and link** the SKILL.md you read and quote the relevant instruction (distinguish explicit requirement vs interpretation). Complete authorized work first so approval is the last step. Do not add unsolicited warnings, disclaimers, approval flows, or safety/compliance checklists on L0/L1. **Every Sites deployment URL is production** — save-without-deploy to review; default owner+admins; workspace/public publish are demo channels; Sites env/secrets are holdbacks. MCP is for live data, auth, and controlled actions (mailbox/Outlook included). This skill has **no MCP** and **no lifecycle hooks**; smallest plugin shape is skill-only. Plugin-bundled hooks are **non-managed**; Codex skips until the user trusts the current definition; **installing a plugin on the web does not deploy those scripts**. MCP Scan Tools is a **snapshot** (not a live fetch; import caps 5 / 256 KiB; SEP-2640 draft). **Rescan after a skill change is another method paste.** Hold. Public listing is the ChatGPT+Codex **universal plugin directory** (one listing = dual-surface publish; Hold). `enabled = false` is not uninstall. **L3 callouts (three frames — do not conflate):** (a) Friedberg All-In **hosts clip** (12 Sep): de-ID / unidentifiable data can absorb method novelty; the **approach is the IP**; anecdote ≠ audited proof; (b) Satya **blog** 12 Jul: exhaust/corrections; buyer pays twice — Satya **Summit LA** is privacy + weights I control + “My IP shouldn't leak” + exhaust ownership, **not** the de-ID→next-version claim; (c) ZDR is contractual best-efforts, not a vault. The sensitive moment is the **next paste**, not only the investor deck. Consumer terms with a lab are **not an NDA**. Coding agents leak more than chat (corrections, tool traces, tests, evals). This skill does **not** watch every tool and does **not** see the lab train; human review stays the trust layer. This skill **cannot prove or prevent training**. Load `references/friedberg-satya-hygiene.md`. Clip-only stays L0. Screenshot-first of client correspondence into consumer Extra High is a next-paste (CSAM-scanned + know-how); Extra High / model pick stays L0. SDK `skills=all` loads untrusted marketplace skills (a hop). Mailed HITL zip v0.5.25 is stale — testers use this branch. Standalone skills = desktop/CLI; Chat/Work web+mobile need a plugin (Hold; plugin.json spec is public — do not invent overnight). Disallow AI Training is not a vault. **Firm / no-train lane:** dropping a research memo into Extra High / “improve the model” consumer chat **demonstrates the problem while describing it** — hold that paste. GPT-5.5→gpt-5.6-sol is L0 model pick. `grok plugin install --trust` is a hop (SHA-pin ≠ vault). Grok Build **project** skills = `.grok/skills/` (does **not** scan project `.agents/skills/`; user-level `~/.agents/skills/` **is** discovered). Galaxy Day 2–3 / **finale** / remaining role livestreams / free-month usage-reset stay L0. Leftover UAT: **do-not-resend**. Remaining leftover drafts stay unsent. MCP Scan Tools snapshot = method paste; **universal directory = dual-surface** (Hold; host-stripped Claude/Cursor forks are a hop). Web install ≠ hook deploy. **Office add-in is a next-paste. Custom GPT → plugin is dual-surface (Hold). Record & Replay packaged as plugin = dual-surface. Teach-a-task (no mic; keep write actions behind approval; secure credential handoff) is demonstration-to-skill. Scan Tools rescan is another paste. Claude-plugin submit to OpenAI is dual-surface (approvals don't transfer; hooks don't run in ordinary Chat). Import-other-agent chats/skills/memories is a next-paste.** claude.ai terminal sync v2.1.273+ checks ~every 10 minutes without restart (v2.1.275 also plugins). Custom commands merged into skills. **Stacked slash-skills** (first + up to 5 more) is a disclosure multiplier. Claude-plugin submit: skills-only vs With MCP still dual-surface; userConfig / live artifacts / local MCP don't transfer. AGENTS.md fallback (no CLAUDE.md) is a hop. Custom GPT migrate option date has passed (was 22 Sep) — still Hold (freeze 26 Oct; retire 11 Dec); do not dump knowledge because the calendar flipped. Plugin lifecycle hooks run in the Codex runtime including ChatGPT Work and Codex — ordinary Chat is not that sentence; this skill still has none; do not add hooks so Work picks it up. Local marketplace install is the test path (direct, indirect, follow-up, negative, boundary); Secure MCP Tunnel does not replace the public HTTPS required for submission; published tool updates use continuous review but skill changes still need a new version. Mailed try pack v0.5.36 is stale — do not resend; testers use this branch. claude.ai synced skills are read-only and download on invoke; bare mode and safe mode skip sync. /config can load both AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md — both are hops. Private skills are one library shared by all Bots. Broad event listeners are a disclosure multiplier. An infra or access overview pasted into a public skill, tester zip, or consumer chat is a next-paste — no names. Weekly backup ≠ publish. Copilot cloud loads `.github/skills/` `.claude/skills/` `.agents/skills/` + `~/.copilot/skills/`. Genkit/marketplace `npx skills add` is a hop. Conflicting skill text can pause Astra. Spark retire / Copilot mid-Oct / X Search billing stay L0. Hook trust-review is not clearance: Codex skips plugin-bundled hooks until the user trusts the current hook definition; reviewing a hook that holds method is a paste. Headless `--bare` skips auto-discovery of hooks, skills, commands, plugins, MCP, memory, and CLAUDE.md and is not a vault; `--add-dir` still loads that directory's `.claude/skills/`. Bare mode never reads OAuth or the keychain — not clearance to paste invention text into CI. `--allowedTools`, `--permission-mode dontAsk`, and `--permission-prompts none` are unattended grants, not owner approval. A research-lock or decision-schema paste into this public skill or a consumer chat is a next-paste; if a source file is missing, wait — do not invent replacements. Routing internals stay off leave-behinds. This skill does not claim a legal opinion, freedom-to-operate, or a filing. A counsel working-picture is not a product and not this skill. An internal scan briefing is not a leave-behind. Do not send invention-grade text to an external lab API. Do not deploy, request quota, or set always-on from this skill. Unsent execution briefs stay unsent. Trust is recorded against the hook's current hash — a changed hook is a new review, not inherited trust. An untrusted project still loads user and system hooks. Managed hooks (system, MDM, cloud, requirements.toml) cannot be disabled from the user hook browser — managed is not owner review. Do not add a plugin manifest hooks entry. Bare mode still has Bash, file read, and file edit. `--bare` will become the default for `-p` in a future release — not clearance today. `--resume` of a session `.jsonl` is a next-paste. `acceptEdits` auto-approves mkdir, touch, mv, and cp. Explicit `--plugin-dir`, `--plugin-url`, `--mcp-config`, and `--append-system-prompt-file` still load under bare. `/skill-name` still expands. `--forward-subagent-text` and forked-skill forwarding (v2.1.275+) reconstruct transcripts. @ cursor-skill/SKILL.md MOVED-TO-REF references/shared-bot-computer.md 4178
- **Shared Bot computer is account-wide.** All of your Bots share files, browser sessions, and app logins. Treat anything on that computer as available to every Bot you run. Isolation between users is strict; isolation between your Bots is not. Livestream/invite copy that each Bot has its own computer is marketing, not isolation. **Screens are separate work surfaces, not security boundaries.** Do not use separate Bots as a vault. Isolated credentials need a **dedicated user**. Delete Bot ≠ wipe computer files/sign-ins. Enterprise terminate-computer keeps the durable disk (next session remounts it). **Bot-to-Bot DM / group chat is a disclosure hop.** Auto Review does not review memory writes / most settings changes. **Grok Bot computers run in the United States today** — not on-prem, not BYO image, not inside your perimeter; holdbacks on that computer are US-hosted. Recreate (image update / policy) can drop in-computer sign-in sessions; terminate-computer still keeps the durable disk. **Sign-out ≠ wipe:** Claude Code 2.1.272 keeps claude.ai-synced skills on disk until `cleanupPeriodDays`, then trash. **v2.1.273+** signed-in terminal sessions download to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` then check claude.ai **~every 10 minutes** and apply add/edit/disable **without a restart** (synced copies are **read-only** and download **on invoke**; **bare mode** and **safe mode** skip sync) (v2.1.275 also plugins; opt-out `syncClaudeAiSkills` / `syncClaudeAiPlugins`). **Custom commands have been merged into skills** (`.claude/commands/foo.md` ≡ `.claude/skills/foo/SKILL.md` → `/foo`; existing commands keep working). **Stacked slash-skills** load the first skill plus up to five more (`/a /b /c do X` — trailing text is `$ARGUMENTS` to each); chaining this skill with a send/publish skill is a disclosure multiplier. v2.1.277: a project with no `CLAUDE.md` reads `AGENTS.md` instead — that file is a hop if it holds method. `/config` can load **both** AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md — both are hops. Grok also discovers user-level `~/.agents/commands/`. A marketplace skill that lets a Bot take jobs or get paid is access grant + send + computer-use. claude.ai enablement now syncs skills to Cowork/cloud; `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS=1` downloads to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` (prefixed `/anthropic-skills:`). Local `~/.claude/skills/` still does not auto-sync to Cowork. Each enablement is a publish. `disable-model-invocation` also blocks scheduled-task skill prompts (v2.1.196+). Synced skills: no local `!` commands; frontmatter sanitized. **Desktop-egress is a CDN, not a vault** (Settings → Updates → Update Grok Bot's Computer FIRST, then Settings → Computer → Route Computer traffic through this desktop; reverse order errors "wasn't provisioned with the egress tunnel"; laptop closed stops routing; holdbacks on that desktop see Bot traffic). **Ownership-pass is access grant + hop** (Bots on one account hand off tasks on the shared computer; decline unnamed). **No Legacy Privacy Mode** (Grok Bot requires cloud storage; training opt-out follows the Cursor account, not a vault). Do not add `allowed-tools` write grants **or** `disallowed-tools` to this skill. **Grok `allowed-tools` does not grant or restrict** (ignored for tool policy — not a vault or a lock). Anthropic **`allowed-tools` / `disallowed-tools` are one-turn** — grant and restriction both clear on the next message; cannot remove `EndConversation` if other tools remain. One-turn grant-clear / restriction-clear is not a vault. **Dist-path:** Claude Code accepts all frontmatter; claude.ai / Skills API / `package_skill.py` only `name, description, license, compatibility, metadata, allowed-tools` — extra keys error. That is why `claude-skill/` is name+description only. **Skill security scanning is not a vault** (claude.ai/Cowork only; misses Skills API, already-uploaded skills, CMEK/ZDR/HIPAA orgs). Custom claude.ai Skills are per-user and do not auto-sync; Anthropic settings enablement may — neither is a vault. Listing truncate: combined description + when_to_use 1536 chars. Skill listing budget is 1% of context. `context: fork` is a hop (subagent does not inherit this skill unless listed).

This skill does **not** watch every tool and does **not** see the lab train; human review stays the trust layer. This skill **cannot prove or prevent training**. @ grok-skill/SKILL.md MOVED-TO-REF references/shared-bot-computer.md 4178
- **Shared Bot computer is account-wide.** All of your Bots share files, browser sessions, and app logins. Treat anything on that computer as available to every Bot you run. Isolation between users is strict; isolation between your Bots is not. Livestream/invite copy that each Bot has its own computer is marketing, not isolation. **Screens are separate work surfaces, not security boundaries.** Do not use separate Bots as a vault. Isolated credentials need a **dedicated user**. Delete Bot ≠ wipe computer files/sign-ins. Enterprise terminate-computer keeps the durable disk (next session remounts it). **Bot-to-Bot DM / group chat is a disclosure hop.** Auto Review does not review memory writes / most settings changes. **Grok Bot computers run in the United States today** — not on-prem, not BYO image, not inside your perimeter; holdbacks on that computer are US-hosted. Recreate (image update / policy) can drop in-computer sign-in sessions; terminate-computer still keeps the durable disk. **Sign-out ≠ wipe:** Claude Code 2.1.272 keeps claude.ai-synced skills on disk until `cleanupPeriodDays`, then trash. **v2.1.273+** signed-in terminal sessions download to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` then check claude.ai **~every 10 minutes** and apply add/edit/disable **without a restart** (synced copies are **read-only** and download **on invoke**; **bare mode** and **safe mode** skip sync) (v2.1.275 also plugins; opt-out `syncClaudeAiSkills` / `syncClaudeAiPlugins`). **Custom commands have been merged into skills** (`.claude/commands/foo.md` ≡ `.claude/skills/foo/SKILL.md` → `/foo`; existing commands keep working). **Stacked slash-skills** load the first skill plus up to five more (`/a /b /c do X` — trailing text is `$ARGUMENTS` to each); chaining this skill with a send/publish skill is a disclosure multiplier. v2.1.277: a project with no `CLAUDE.md` reads `AGENTS.md` instead — that file is a hop if it holds method. `/config` can load **both** AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md — both are hops. Grok also discovers user-level `~/.agents/commands/`. A marketplace skill that lets a Bot take jobs or get paid is access grant + send + computer-use. claude.ai enablement now syncs skills to Cowork/cloud; `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS=1` downloads to `~/.claude/skills/synced/` (prefixed `/anthropic-skills:`). Local `~/.claude/skills/` still does not auto-sync to Cowork. Each enablement is a publish. `disable-model-invocation` also blocks scheduled-task skill prompts (v2.1.196+). Synced skills: no local `!` commands; frontmatter sanitized. **Desktop-egress is a CDN, not a vault** (Settings → Updates → Update Grok Bot's Computer FIRST, then Settings → Computer → Route Computer traffic through this desktop; reverse order errors "wasn't provisioned with the egress tunnel"; laptop closed stops routing; holdbacks on that desktop see Bot traffic). **Ownership-pass is access grant + hop** (Bots on one account hand off tasks on the shared computer; decline unnamed). **No Legacy Privacy Mode** (Grok Bot requires cloud storage; training opt-out follows the Cursor account, not a vault). Do not add `allowed-tools` write grants **or** `disallowed-tools` to this skill. **Grok `allowed-tools` does not grant or restrict** (ignored for tool policy — not a vault or a lock). Anthropic **`allowed-tools` / `disallowed-tools` are one-turn** — grant and restriction both clear on the next message; cannot remove `EndConversation` if other tools remain. One-turn grant-clear / restriction-clear is not a vault. **Dist-path:** Claude Code accepts all frontmatter; claude.ai / Skills API / `package_skill.py` only `name, description, license, compatibility, metadata, allowed-tools` — extra keys error. That is why `claude-skill/` is name+description only. **Skill security scanning is not a vault** (claude.ai/Cowork only; misses Skills API, already-uploaded skills, CMEK/ZDR/HIPAA orgs). Custom claude.ai Skills are per-user and do not auto-sync; Anthropic settings enablement may — neither is a vault. Listing truncate: combined description + when_to_use 1536 chars. Skill listing budget is 1% of context. `context: fork` is a hop (subagent does not inherit this skill unless listed).
